dir=\"auto\"\u003eOne button. Infinite web.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003c/blockquote\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eRoam is a community-driven web discovery platform that helps you find genuinely interesting pages matched to your interests. Press one button and land on a curated page voted on by real users, not ranked by engagement metrics or algorithmic preference.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003chr\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"markdown-heading\" dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003ch2 tabindex=\"-1\" class=\"heading-element\" dir=\"auto\"\u003eWhat is Roam?\u003c/h2\u003e\u003ca id=\"user-content-what-is-roam\" class=\"anchor\" aria-label=\"Permalink: What is Roam?\" href=\"#what-is-roam\"\u003e\u003csvg data-component=\"Octicon\" class=\"octicon octicon-link\" viewBox=\"0 0 16 16\" version=\"1.1\" width=\"16\" height=\"16\" aria-hidden=\"true\"\u003e\u003cpath d=\"m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z\"\u003e\u003c/path\u003e\u003c/svg\u003e\u003c/a\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eThe internet is vast and wonderful, but discovery has been broken for years. Algorithmic feeds optimize for engagement, not quality. Bookmarking services feel like digital graveyards. Search requires knowing what you're looking for.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eRoam brings back the joy of stumbling — the simple act of pressing a button and landing somewhere unexpected that still aligns with what you care about. No feeds. No algorithms optimized to keep you scrolling. No noise. Just real pages, real user votes, and real serendipity.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003chr\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"markdown-heading\" dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003ch2 tabindex=\"-1\" class=\"heading-element\" dir=\"auto\"\u003eWhy Roam?\u003c/h2\u003e\u003ca id=\"user-content-why-roam\" class=\"anchor\" aria-label=\"Permalink: Why Roam?\" href=\"#why-roam\"\u003e\u003csvg data-component=\"Octicon\" class=\"octicon octicon-link\" viewBox=\"0 0 16 16\" version=\"1.1\" width=\"16\" height=\"16\" aria-hidden=\"true\"\u003e\u003cpath d=\"m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z\"\u003e\u003c/path\u003e\u003c/svg\u003e\u003c/a\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eThe problem with existing discovery tools:\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cul dir=\"auto\"\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eAlgorithmic feeds\u003c/strong\u003e — optimize for time-on-site, not quality\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eSearch engines\u003c/strong\u003e — require you to know what you're looking for\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eStatic curated lists\u003c/strong\u003e — curators can't match millions of user interests\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eRandom links\u003c/strong\u003e — no personalization, no quality signal\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eSocial media\u003c/strong\u003e — feeds designed for engagement, not discovery\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003c/ul\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eRoam solves this with:\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003col dir=\"auto\"\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eCommunity voting\u003c/strong\u003e — real users rate pages, not algorithms\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eTransparent personalization\u003c/strong\u003e — your interests control what you see\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eQuality filter\u003c/strong\u003e — pages with sustained negative votes are deprioritized\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eFresh content\u003c/strong\u003e — users continuously submit new URLs\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eMulti-platform\u003c/strong\u003e — web, mobile, and browser extension all sync\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eZero ads, zero tracking\u003c/strong\u003e — Roam doesn't profit from your attention\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003c/ol\u003e\n\u003chr\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"markdown-heading\" dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003ch2 tabindex=\"-1\" Roam is the discovery algorithm. Unlike recommendation engines that optimize for engagement:\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cul dir=\"auto\"\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eRoam button\u003c/strong\u003e — one tap/click delivers a curated page matching your active interest filters\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eInterest categories\u003c/strong\u003e — select categories at signup and adjust at any time (Arts, Science, Tech, News, Gaming, etc.)\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eCommunity-voted ranking\u003c/strong\u003e — pages are ranked by real user votes, not engagement metrics\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eDomain diversity\u003c/strong\u003e — discovery engine spreads results across different domains to avoid filter bubbles\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003cstrong\u003ePersonalized pool\u003c/strong\u003e — each user gets a candidate pool pre-filtered to their interests, then a single page is randomly selected and served\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eWilson score ranking\u003c/strong\u003e — community votes use statistical methods to surface genuinely good content, not just popular content\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eContextual discovery\u003c/strong\u003e — \"Roam within site\" mode lets you stay on a specific domain and discover more pages from it\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eLanguage filtering\u003c/strong\u003e — filter results by language preference\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eAdult content filter\u003c/strong\u003e — option to exclude NSFW content\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003c/ul\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"markdown-heading\" dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003ch3 tabindex=\"-1\" class=\"heading-element\" dir=\"auto\"\u003eTransparent Personalization\u003c/h3\u003e\u003ca id=\"user-content-transparent-personalization\" class=\"anchor\" aria-label=\"Permalink: Transparent Personalization\" href=\"#transparent-personalization\"\u003e\u003csvg data-component=\"Octicon\" class=\"octicon octicon-link\" viewBox=\"0 0 16 16\" version=\"1.1\" width=\"16\" height=\"16\" aria-hidden=\"true\"\u003e\u003cpath d=\"m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 2.83Z\"\u003e\u003c/path\u003e\u003c/svg\u003e\u003c/a\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eYour recommendations are controlled by just two mechanisms:\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003col dir=\"auto\"\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eTopic affinity\u003c/strong\u003e — topics you thumbs-up more often appear slightly more often in your results (up to +30% weight boost). Topics you down-vote are not hidden — just weighted a little less, preserving serendipity.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eDomain muting\u003c/strong\u003e — if you thumbs-down 2 or more pages from the same website, that site is automatically muted for 30 days. No manual blocklist to manage; the mute expires on its own.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003c/ol\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eCommunity-level filtering also applies:\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cul dir=\"auto\"\u003e\n\u003cli\u003ePages with sustained negative votes from the whole user base are deprioritized for everyone\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eSpam and duplicates are removed by moderators\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003ePaywalled content can be flagged and deprioritized\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003c/ul\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"markdown-heading\" dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003ch3 tabindex=\"-1\" class=\"heading-element\" dir=\"auto\"\u003eContent Management\u003c/h3\u003e\u003ca id=\"user-content-content-management\" class=\"anchor\" aria-label=\"Permalink: Content dir=\"auto\"\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eURL submission\u003c/strong\u003e — any user can submit a new page to Roam\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eCategory tagging\u003c/strong\u003e — submitters tag pages with relevant categories\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eModerator queue\u003c/strong\u003e — submissions are queued for community review\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eDuplicate detection\u003c/strong\u003e — system prevents the same URL from being added twice\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eDomain tracking\u003c/strong\u003e — track which domains contribute most pages and which have lowest user satisfaction\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eOrganic growth\u003c/strong\u003e — content pool expands only through user contributions\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003c/ul\u003e\n\u003cdiv dir=\"auto\"\u003ePurpose-built for the discovery workflow:\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cul dir=\"auto\"\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eNon-intrusive design\u003c/strong\u003e — click popup, roam, rate, close. Nothing is injected into pages.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eRoam button\u003c/strong\u003e — get a new page instantly\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eThumbs up / down\u003c/strong\u003e — rate pages while browsing\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eCategory/collection controls\u003c/strong\u003e — save pages to collections or filter by category\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eBrowser-tab integration\u003c/strong\u003e — detects and rates the URL you're currently viewing\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eQueue prefetching\u003c/strong\u003e — fetches next 3 pages in background for instant loading\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eOffline support\u003c/strong\u003e — works even if you lose network momentarily\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eChrome (MV3) + Firefox (MV3)\u003c/strong\u003e — compatible with modern browser extension standards\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eAutomatic updates\u003c/strong\u003e — stays in sync with web platform (ratings, follows, collections)\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003c/ul\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"markdown-heading\" dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003ch3 tabindex=\"-1\" class=\"heading-element\" queue\u003c/strong\u003e — save pages for later without network\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003cstrong\u003ePush notifications\u003c/strong\u003e — get notified of activity (followers, free tier for cost-effective, serverless operation:\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eDatabase Layer\u003c/strong\u003e\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cul dir=\"auto\"\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003cstrong\u003ePostgreSQL\u003c/strong\u003e — Supabase free tier (500 MB storage, sufficient for millions of discovery records)\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eRow-Level Security (RLS)\u003c/strong\u003e — all access control enforced at the database level; no application-layer auth needed\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eKey tables:\u003c/strong\u003e\n\u003cul dir=\"auto\"\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003ccode\u003eurls\u003c/code\u003e — discovered pages (url, title, description, category, community votes, wilson_score)\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003ccode\u003ecollections\u003c/code\u003e — user-created collections (name, description, visibility, owner)\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003ccode\u003ecollection_items\u003c/code\u003e — pages saved to collections\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003ccode\u003eprofiles\u003c/code\u003e — user profiles (email, interests, settings, follower count)\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003ccode\u003efollows\u003c/code\u003e — follow relationships between users\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003ccode\u003eratings\u003c/code\u003e — user votes (url_id, user_id, vote direction, timestamp)\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003ccode\u003emoderation_queue\u003c/code\u003e — submitted URLs pending approval\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003ccode\u003eseen_urls\u003c/code\u003e — track which pages a user has already rated (prevents duplicates)\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003c/ul\u003e\n\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003c/ul\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eAuthentication\u003c/strong\u003e\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cul dir=\"auto\"\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eSupabase Auth\u003c/strong\u003e — email/password + Google OAuth out-of-box\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eSession persistence\u003c/strong\u003e — browser-based session storage with auto-refresh\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eAdmin role\u003c/strong\u003e — users with \u003ccode\u003eis_admin=true\u003c/code\u003e can moderate submissions\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003c/ul\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eServer-Side Logic — Edge Functions (Deno)\u003c/strong\u003e\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cul dir=\"auto\"\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eroam(category_filter?, exclude_domain?)\u003c/strong\u003e — core algorithm RPC function:\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cul dir=\"auto\"\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eFilters candidate pool by user's categories and muted domains\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eApplies personalization weights (topic affinity + domain muting)\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eSamples from remaining candidates using Wilson score ranking\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eReturns single URL with metadata (title, description, category, OG image)\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eFalls back gracefully when pool is exhausted\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003c/ul\u003e\n\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003cstrong\u003erate(url_id, vote)\u003c/strong\u003e — record user votes:\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cul dir=\"auto\"\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eValidates URL ownership and user permissions\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eUpdates vote count and recalculates Wilson score\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eRecords in \u003ccode\u003eratings\u003c/code\u003e table for personalization\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eTriggers domain-mute logic if threshold reached\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003c/ul\u003e\n\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003cstrong\u003esubmit-url(url, category)\u003c/strong\u003e — submit new pages:\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cul dir=\"auto\"\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eNormalizes URL (removes tracking params, www prefix, fragments)\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eChecks for duplicates\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eAdds to moderation queue\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eNotifies moderators\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003c/ul\u003e\n\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003cstrong\u003ecollection(action, ...)\u003c/strong\u003e — manage saved pages:\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cul dir=\"auto\"\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eCreate collections\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eAdd/remove URLs from collections\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eList collections with item counts\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eShare collections publicly\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003c/ul\u003e\n\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003cstrong\u003efollow(action, user_id)\u003c/strong\u003e — manage follows:\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cul dir=\"auto\"\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eFollow/unfollow users\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eList followers and following\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eCalculate follower counts for profiles\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003c/ul\u003e\n\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003cstrong\u003elog-failed-urls(failed_urls)\u003c/strong\u003e — batch error logging:\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cul dir=\"auto\"\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eRecords URLs that failed validation dir=\"auto\"\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003ccode\u003eurls\u003c/code\u003e table indexed on \u003ccode\u003ecategory_id\u003c/code\u003e and \u003ccode\u003eapproved\u003c/code\u003e for fast filtering\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003ccode\u003eratings\u003c/code\u003e table indexed on \u003ccode\u003eurl_id\u003c/code\u003e for vote aggregation\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003ccode\u003eseen_urls\u003c/code\u003e tracks viewed pages to prevent repeats\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eWilson score algorithm ranks pages statistically correctly (no bias toward new or old)\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003c/ul\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eScalability\u003c/strong\u003e\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cul dir=\"auto\"\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eNormalized schema prevents data duplication\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eRLS policies prevent data leakage between users\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eForeign keys enforce referential integrity\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eMaterialized queries cache expensive aggregations (follower counts, collection sizes)\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003c/ul\u003e\n\u003chr\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"markdown-heading\" dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003ch2 tabindex=\"-1\" class=\"heading-element\" dir=\"auto\"\u003eRepository  └── ... data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content=\"# 1. Clone repository\ngit clone https://github.com/seito/roam.git\ncd roam\n\n# 2. Create free Supabase project at https://supabase.com\n# Copy your project URL and anon key\n\n# 3. Backend setup\nsupabase login\nsupabase link --project-ref YOUR_PROJECT_REF\nsupabase db push          # Applies all SQL migrations\nsupabase functions deploy # Deploys Edge Functions\n\n# 4. Extension setup\ncd extension\npnpm install\npnpm build               # Outputs to dist/\n# Load unpacked in Chrome: chrome://extensions → Developer mode → Load unpacked → select dist/\"\u003e\u003cpre\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e#\u003c/span\u003e 1. Create free Supabase project at https://supabase.com\u003c/span\u003e\n\u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e#\u003c/span\u003e Copy your project URL and anon key\u003c/span\u003e\n\n\u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e#\u003c/span\u003e 3. 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z\"\u003e\u003c/path\u003e\u003c/svg\u003e\u003c/a\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eThe \u003ccode\u003eroam()\u003c/code\u003e function uses several optimization techniques:\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003col dir=\"auto\"\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003cstrong\u003ePre-filtered candidate pool\u003c/strong\u003e — only candidates matching user's categories are considered\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eDomain muting optimization\u003c/strong\u003e — muted domains are excluded at query time\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eStatistical ranking (Wilson score)\u003c/strong\u003e — prevents new/popular bias; pages with 5 thumbs-up and 100 votes rank correctly\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eEfficient sampling\u003c/strong\u003e — uses PostgreSQL \u003ccode\u003eTABLESAMPLE BERNOULLI\u003c/code\u003e for large tables\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eConnection pooling\u003c/strong\u003e — Supabase manages connection reuse automatically\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003c/ol\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eExpected latency:\u003c/strong\u003e\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cul dir=\"auto\"\u003e\n\u003cli\u003ep50: ~100ms\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003ep95: ~500ms\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003ep99: ~2s (when database is under high load)\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003c/ul\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"markdown-heading\" dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003ch3 tabindex=\"-1\" background, instant response to user clicks\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eLazy script loading\u003c/strong\u003e — content scripts load only on tabs with matching domain patterns\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eStorage optimization\u003c/strong\u003e — queue stored compressed in chrome.storage.local\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eMinimal permissions\u003c/strong\u003e — extension only accesses storage and makes network requests\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003c/ul\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eExpected memory usage:\u003c/strong\u003e\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cul dir=\"auto\"\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eIdle: ~5 file","ghDesktopPath":"x-github-client://openRepo/https://github.com/seven-lynx/Roam?branch=main\u0026filepath=README.md","isGitLfs":false,"onBranch":true,"shortPath":"980190d","siteNavLoginPath":"/login?return_to=https%3A%2F%2Fgithub.com%2Fseven-lynx%2FRoam%2Fblob%2Fmain%2FREADME.md","isCSV":false,"isRichtext":true,"lineInfo":{"truncatedLoc":"997","truncatedSloc":"752"},"mode":"file"},"image":false,"isCodeownersFile":null,"isPlain":false,"isValidLegacyIssueTemplate":false,"isIssueTemplate":false,"isDiscussionTemplate":false,"language":"Markdown","languageID":222,"large":false,"planSupportInfo":{"repoIsFork":null,"repoOwnedByCurrentUser":null,"requestFullPath":"/seven-lynx/Roam/blob/main/README.md","showFreeOrgGatedFeatureMessage":null,"showPlanSupportBanner":null,"upgradeDataAttributes":null,"upgradePath":null},"publishBannersInfo":{"dismissActionNoticePath":"/settings/dismiss-notice/publish_action_from_dockerfile","releasePath":"/seven-lynx/Roam/releases/new?marketplace=true","showPublishActionBanner":false},"rawBlobUrl":"https://github.com/seven-lynx/Roam/raw/refs/heads/main/README.md","renderImageOrRaw":false,"shortPath":null,"symbolsEnabled":true,"tabSize":4,"topBannersInfo":{"overridingGlobalFundingFile":false,"globalPreferredFundingPath":null,"showInvalidCitationWarning":false,"citationHelpUrl":"https://docs.github.com/github/creating-cloning-and-archiving-repositories/creating-a-repository-on-github/about-citation-files","actionsOnboardingTip":null},"truncated":false,"viewable":true,"workflowRedirectUrl":null},"copilotInfo":null,"copilotAccessAllowed":true,"copilotSpacesEnabled":true,"modelsAccessAllowed":false,"modelsRepoIntegrationEnabled":false,"isMarketplaceEnabled":true},"codeViewBlobLayoutRoute.StyledBlob":{"rawLines":["# Roam — Rediscover the Web","","\u003e One button. Infinite web.","","Roam is a community-driven web discovery platform that helps you find genuinely interesting pages matched to your interests. Press one button and land on a curated page voted on by real users, not ranked by engagement metrics or algorithmic preference.","","---","","## What is Roam?","","The internet is vast and wonderful, but discovery has been broken for years. Algorithmic feeds optimize for engagement, not quality. Bookmarking services feel like digital graveyards. Search requires knowing what you're looking for.","","Roam brings back the joy of stumbling — the simple act of pressing a button and landing somewhere unexpected that still aligns with what you care about. No feeds. No algorithms optimized to keep you scrolling. No noise. Just real pages, real user votes, and real serendipity.","","---","","## Why Roam?","","The problem with existing discovery tools:","- **Algorithmic feeds** — optimize for time-on-site, not quality","- **Search engines** — require you to know what you're looking for","- **Static curated lists** — curators can't match millions of user interests","- **Random links** — no personalization, no quality signal","- **Social media** — feeds designed for engagement, not discovery","","Roam solves this with:","1. **Community voting** — real users rate pages, not algorithms","2. **Transparent personalization** — your interests control what you see","3. **Quality filter** — pages with sustained negative votes are deprioritized","4. **Fresh content** — users continuously submit new URLs","5. **Multi-platform** — web, mobile, and browser extension all sync","6. **Zero ads, zero tracking** — Roam doesn't profit from your attention","","---","","## Platform Status","","| Component | Status | Audience |","|-----------|--------|----------|","| Supabase Backend | ✅ Live | All platforms |","| Web Platform | ✅ Live | Desktop/tablet browsers |","| Browser Extension | ✅ Live | Chrome \u0026 Firefox users |","| Android App | In development | Mobile Android users |","","","","---","","## Core Features","","### Discovery Engine","The heart of Roam is the discovery algorithm. Unlike recommendation engines that optimize for engagement:","","- **Roam button** — one tap/click delivers a curated page matching your active interest filters","- **Interest categories** — select categories at signup and adjust at any time (Arts, Science, Tech, News, Gaming, etc.)","- **Community-voted ranking** — pages are ranked by real user votes, not engagement metrics","- **Domain diversity** — discovery engine spreads results across different domains to avoid filter bubbles","- **Personalized pool** — each user gets a candidate pool pre-filtered to their interests, then a single page is randomly selected and served","- **Wilson score ranking** — community votes use statistical methods to surface genuinely good content, not just popular content","- **Contextual discovery** — \"Roam within site\" mode lets you stay on a specific domain and discover more pages from it","- **Language filtering** — filter results by language preference","- **Adult content filter** — option to exclude NSFW content","","### Transparent Personalization","","Your recommendations are controlled by just two mechanisms:","","1. **Topic affinity** — topics you thumbs-up more often appear slightly more often in your results (up to +30% weight boost). Topics you down-vote are not hidden — just weighted a little less, preserving serendipity.","","2. **Domain muting** — if you thumbs-down 2 or more pages from the same website, that site is automatically muted for 30 days. No manual blocklist to manage; the mute expires on its own.","","Community-level filtering also applies:","- Pages with sustained negative votes from the whole user base are deprioritized for everyone","- Spam and duplicates are removed by moderators","- Paywalled content can be flagged and deprioritized","","### Content Management","","Users drive the content pool:","- **URL submission** — any user can submit a new page to Roam","- **Category tagging** — submitters tag pages with relevant categories","- **Moderator queue** — submissions are queued for community review","- **Duplicate detection** — system prevents the same URL from being added twice","- **Domain tracking** — track which domains contribute most pages and which have lowest user satisfaction","- **Organic growth** — content pool expands only through user contributions","","### User Accounts \u0026 Profiles","","- **Authentication** — email + password or Google OAuth","- **Interest profile** — select categories that match your interests","- **Discovery history** — track what pages you've already rated","- **Personal blocklist** — manually mute domains for 30 days","- **Saved collections** — create public or private collections to save pages","- **User profile** — see your stats: pages rated, collections created, followers","- **Account settings** — manage auth, privacy, notifications","","### Social Layer","","- **Follow other users** — see their activity and recommendation history","- **Shared collections** — create public collections to share with followers","- **User profiles** — browse what pages other users have rated highly","- **Activity notifications** — get notified when users you follow like a page you submitted","- **Reputation** — track how many followers you have and pages you've contributed","","### Browser Extension","","Purpose-built for the discovery workflow:","","- **Non-intrusive design** — click popup, roam, rate, close. Nothing is injected into pages.","- **Roam button** — get a new page instantly","- **Thumbs up / down** — rate pages while browsing","- **Category/collection controls** — save pages to collections or filter by category","- **Browser-tab integration** — detects and rates the URL you're currently viewing","- **Queue prefetching** — fetches next 3 pages in background for instant loading","- **Offline support** — works even if you lose network momentarily","- **Chrome (MV3) + Firefox (MV3)** — compatible with modern browser extension standards","- **Automatic updates** — stays in sync with web platform (ratings, follows, collections)","","### Android App","","Optimized for mobile discovery:","","- **In-app browser** — read pages directly in the app without leaving","- **Swipe gestures** — swipe right to like, swipe left to skip, swipe down for details","- **Quick-add to collections** — save pages with one tap","- **Offline reading queue** — save pages for later without network","- **Push notifications** — get notified of activity (followers, collections, submissions)","- **Dark mode** — eye-friendly nighttime reading","- **Android 8.0+ support** — works on older devices","- **Material Design 3** — native Android look and feel using Jetpack Compose","","### Web Platform","","Full-featured discovery and account management:","","- **Discovery interface** — web version of roam, with advanced filters","- **Account management** — edit profile, interests, collections, followers","- **URL submission** — submit new pages directly","- **Moderation dashboard** — (admin) review and approve submissions","- **Analytics dashboard** — view platform stats and trends","- **Responsive design** — works on desktop and tablet","","---","","## Architecture \u0026 Tech Stack","","### Backend Architecture — Supabase (PostgreSQL + Edge Functions)","","Built entirely on Supabase's free tier for cost-effective, serverless operation:","","**Database Layer**","- **PostgreSQL** — Supabase free tier (500 MB storage, sufficient for millions of discovery records)","- **Row-Level Security (RLS)** — all access control enforced at the database level; no application-layer auth needed","- **Key tables:**","  - `urls` — discovered pages (url, title, description, category, community votes, wilson_score)","  - `collections` — user-created collections (name, description, visibility, owner)","  - `collection_items` — pages saved to collections","  - `profiles` — user profiles (email, interests, settings, follower count)","  - `follows` — follow relationships between users","  - `ratings` — user votes (url_id, user_id, vote direction, timestamp)","  - `moderation_queue` — submitted URLs pending approval","  - `seen_urls` — track which pages a user has already rated (prevents duplicates)","","**Authentication**","- **Supabase Auth** — email/password + Google OAuth out-of-box","- **Session persistence** — browser-based session storage with auto-refresh","- **Admin role** — users with `is_admin=true` can moderate submissions","","**Server-Side Logic — Edge Functions (Deno)**","- **roam(category_filter?, exclude_domain?)** — core algorithm RPC function:","  - Filters candidate pool by user's categories and muted domains","  - Applies personalization weights (topic affinity + domain muting)","  - Samples from remaining candidates using Wilson score ranking","  - Returns single URL with metadata (title, description, category, OG image)","  - Falls back gracefully when pool is exhausted","","- **rate(url_id, vote)** — record user votes:","  - Validates URL ownership and user permissions","  - Updates vote count and recalculates Wilson score","  - Records in `ratings` table for personalization","  - Triggers domain-mute logic if threshold reached","","- **submit-url(url, category)** — submit new pages:","  - Normalizes URL (removes tracking params, www prefix, fragments)","  - Checks for duplicates","  - Adds to moderation queue","  - Notifies moderators","","- **collection(action, ...)** — manage saved pages:","  - Create collections","  - Add/remove URLs from collections","  - List collections with item counts","  - Share collections publicly","","- **follow(action, user_id)** — manage follows:","  - Follow/unfollow users","  - List followers and following","  - Calculate follower counts for profiles","","- **log-failed-urls(failed_urls)** — batch error logging:","  - Records URLs that failed validation (for moderation)","  - Implements retry logic with exponential backoff","  - Captures to Sentry for monitoring","","**API Gateway \u0026 CORS**","- All Edge Functions use shared CORS headers (`_shared/cors.ts`)","- Authentication via Supabase bearer token in `Authorization` header","- All endpoints are POST or POST+OPTIONS (for CORS preflight)","","### Browser Extension (`/extension`)","","**Architecture**","- **Manifest V3** — modern extension standard, works on Chrome and Firefox","- **Service Worker** — background.ts handles all logic:","  - Persistent storage of auth state and queue","  - Message routing from popup and content scripts","  - Background sync (queue refilling, failed URL retries)","- **Popup UI** — popup.ts handles user interface","- **Content Script** — callback.ts detects current page and enables rating","","**Build \u0026 Distribution**","- **Build tool** — esbuild with TypeScript","- **Source maps** — uploaded to Sentry with @sentry/esbuild-plugin","- **Chrome** — Chrome Web Store ($5 one-time)","- **Firefox** — Firefox Add-ons (free)","- **Sentry integration** — monitors crashes and errors from all users","","**Queue Management**","- **Hot queue** — 3 pre-fetched URLs ready to display instantly","- **Warming queue** — 5 additional URLs being fetched in background","- **Prefetch loop** — continuously maintains queue above threshold","- **Persistent storage** — chrome.storage.local survives restarts","- **Retry logic** — failed fetches retry with exponential backoff","","**Key Modules**","- `background.ts` — service worker, message dispatcher, auth handler","- `popup.ts` — UI logic, roam button, rating buttons, category filter","- `callback.ts` — detects current page, enables rating of known URLs","- `lib/queue.ts` — queue state management and batch retry","- `lib/queueManager.ts` — background loops for validation and refill","- `lib/supabase.ts` — Supabase client factory with auth state","","### Android App (`/android`)","","**Language \u0026 UI**","- **Kotlin** — type-safe, concise Android development","- **Jetpack Compose** — modern declarative UI framework","- **Material Design 3** — latest Android design system with theming","- **Navigation Compose** — type-safe navigation between screens","","**Networking \u0026 Authentication**","- **Supabase Kotlin client** — official SDK for database and auth","- **Google Sign-In** — integrated into Supabase Auth","- **Session persistence** — auto-refresh tokens via Supabase","","**Key Features**","- **In-app WebView** — render discovered pages within the app","- **Swipe gestures** — intuitive right (like) / left (skip) / down (details) interactions","- **Offline queue** — save pages with content snapshot for offline reading","- **Push notifications** — Firebase Cloud Messaging for activity alerts","- **Min SDK 26 (Android 8.0)** — supports older devices","- **Target SDK 34 (Android 14)** — modern API features","","**Key Screens**","- Roam screen — display current URL, swipe controls, metadata","- Collections screen — create and manage saved page collections","- Profile screen — view account, interests, followers, statistics","- Following screen — discover and follow other users","- Settings screen — auth, notifications, content filtering","","### Web Platform (`/web`)","","**Framework \u0026 Build**","- **Next.js 15** — React framework with server-side rendering","- **TypeScript** — type-safe full-stack code","- **Tailwind CSS** — utility-first styling","- **Vercel deployment** — free tier hosting with auto-deployment on git push","","**Key Pages**","- Home — roam interface with discovery","- Profile — view user account and statistics","- Collections — browse public collections","- Submit URL — contribute new pages","- Moderation (admin) — review and approve submissions","- Analytics (admin) — view platform statistics","","**Authentication \u0026 State**","- **Supabase Auth** — user sessions and permissions","- **Server-side sessions** — secure auth via Supabase SSR library","- **Client-side persistence** — React Query for state management","","### Database Design Highlights","","**Efficient Discovery**","- `urls` table indexed on `category_id` and `approved` for fast filtering","- `ratings` table indexed on `url_id` for vote aggregation","- `seen_urls` tracks viewed pages to prevent repeats","- Wilson score algorithm ranks pages statistically correctly (no bias toward new or old)","","**Scalability**","- Normalized schema prevents data duplication","- RLS policies prevent data leakage between users","- Foreign keys enforce referential integrity","- Materialized queries cache expensive aggregations (follower counts, collection sizes)","","---","","## Repository Structure","","```","roam/","├── supabase/                   # Backend database \u0026 serverless functions","│   ├── config.toml            # Supabase local dev config","│   ├── migrations/            # SQL migrations (initialize schema)","│   │   ├── 20260423000000_initial.sql              # Core tables: users, urls, collections","│   │   ├── 20260424000000_schema_improvements.sql  # Indexes and constraints","│   │   ├── 20260426000000_fix_roam_null_subcategories.sql  # Category handling","│   │   └── ... (40+ migrations)                    # Performance tuning, RPC functions","│   └── functions/              # Deno Edge Functions (TypeScript)","│       ├── roam/              # GET /functions/v1/roam — core discovery algorithm","│       ├── rate/              # POST /functions/v1/rate — record votes","│       ├── submit-url/        # POST /functions/v1/submit-url — contribute URLs","│       ├── collection/        # POST /functions/v1/collection — manage collections","│       ├── follow/            # POST /functions/v1/follow — manage follows","│       ├── profile/           # GET /functions/v1/profile — user profile data","│       ├── log-failed-urls/   # POST /functions/v1/log-failed-urls — error tracking","│       └── _shared/           # Shared utilities","│           ├── cors.ts        # CORS headers for all endpoints","│           └── auth.ts        # Auth helper functions","│","├── web/                        # Next.js web application (Vercel)","│   ├── src/","│   │   ├── app/              # Next.js app directory","│   │   │   ├── page.tsx      # Home page (roam interface)","│   │   │   ├── profile/      # User profile pages","│   │   │   ├── collections/  # Collection browsing","│   │   │   └── submit/       # URL submission form","│   │   ├── components/        # React components","│   │   ├── lib/              # Utility functions","│   │   └── middleware.ts      # Auth middleware","│   ├── package.json          # Dependencies and build scripts","│   ├── next.config.ts        # Next.js configuration","│   ├── tsconfig.json         # TypeScript config","│   └── .env.local            # Supabase credentials","│","├── extension/                  # Browser extension (Chrome + Firefox)","│   ├── src/","│   │   ├── background/       # Service worker (background.ts)","│   │   ├── popup/            # Popup UI (popup.ts, popup.css, popup.html)","│   │   ├── callback/         # Content script (callback.ts)","│   │   ├── lib/              # Shared utilities","│   │   │   ├── queue.ts      # Queue state and batch retry","│   │   │   ├── queueManager.ts  # Background loops","│   │   │   └── supabase.ts   # Supabase client factory","│   │   └── types/            # TypeScript type definitions","│   ├── icons/                # Extension icons (16x16, 32x32, 48x48, 128x128)","│   ├── dist/                 # Compiled output (ignored in git)","│   ├── dist-firefox/         # Firefox-specific bundle","│   ├── manifest.json         # Extension manifest (Chrome)","│   ├── manifest.firefox.json # Extension manifest (Firefox)","│   ├── build.mjs             # esbuild configuration","│   ├── package.json          # Dependencies","│   └── tsconfig.json         # TypeScript config","│","├── android/                    # Android app (Kotlin + Compose)","│   ├── app/src/main/kotlin/com/roam","│   │   ├── MainActivity.kt    # Entry point","│   │   ├── ui/               # Jetpack Compose screens","│   │   ├── viewmodel/        # MVVM ViewModels","│   │   └── data/             # Repository and Supabase client","│   ├── build.gradle.kts      # Kotlin DSL build config","│   ├── gradle.properties     # Gradle settings","│   ├── local.properties      # Local secrets (not in git)","│   └── settings.gradle.kts   # Gradle subproject config","│","├── scripts/                    # One-off utility scripts","│   ├── seed-*.js             # Import URLs from public sources (Wikipedia, Hacker News, etc.)","│   ├── _test-*.mjs           # Quick tests of seed scripts","│   └── resize-icons.ps1      # Icon resizing script","│","├── PLANNING.md               # Long-term roadmap","├── ROADMAP.md                # Public build tasks \u0026 progress tracking","├── LICENSE                   # MIT license","└── README.md                # This file","```","","---","","## Development Setup","","### Prerequisites","","- **Node.js 20+** — for extension build and web platform","- **pnpm 9+** — fast, space-efficient package manager (or npm/yarn if preferred)","- **Supabase CLI** — `npm install -g supabase` or `brew install supabase`","- **Android Studio** — Ladybug version or newer (for Android development)","- **Git** — version control","","### Quick Start (5 minutes)","","```bash","# 1. Clone repository","git clone https://github.com/seito/roam.git","cd roam","","# 2. Create free Supabase project at https://supabase.com","# Copy your project URL and anon key","","# 3. Backend setup","supabase login","supabase link --project-ref YOUR_PROJECT_REF","supabase db push          # Applies all SQL migrations","supabase functions deploy # Deploys Edge Functions","","# 4. Extension setup","cd extension","pnpm install","pnpm build               # Outputs to dist/","# Load unpacked in Chrome: chrome://extensions → Developer mode → Load unpacked → select dist/","```","","### Backend (Supabase)","","**One-time setup:**","","1. Create a free account at [supabase.com](https://supabase.com)","2. Create a new project (any region)","3. Get your project URL and anon key from Settings → API Keys","","**Local development:**","","```bash","cd supabase","","# Authenticate with Supabase","supabase login","","# Link to your project","supabase link --project-ref YOUR_PROJECT_REF","","# Apply all database migrations","supabase db push","","# Deploy Edge Functions","supabase functions deploy roam","supabase functions deploy rate","supabase functions deploy submit-url","supabase functions deploy collection","supabase functions deploy follow","supabase functions deploy profile","supabase functions deploy log-failed-urls","","# Run local dev server (optional)","supabase start  # Starts local PostgreSQL, auth server, and functions","supabase stop","```","","**Verifying the backend:**","","```bash","# List deployed functions","supabase functions list","","# Check Edge Function logs","supabase functions download roam","supabase edge-functions --help","```","","### Browser Extension","","**Development:**","","```bash","cd extension","","# Install dependencies","pnpm install","","# Build and watch for changes","pnpm watch","","# Or build once","pnpm build","","# Output: dist/ (Chrome) and dist-firefox/ (Firefox)","```","","**Loading in Chrome:**","","1. Open `chrome://extensions`","2. Enable \"Developer mode\" (top right)","3. Click \"Load unpacked\"","4. Select the `dist/` folder","5. Extension appears in your toolbar","","**Loading in Firefox:**","","1. Open `about:debugging`","2. Click \"This Firefox\"","3. Click \"Load Temporary Add-on\"","4. Select `dist-firefox/manifest.json`","","**Debugging:**","","```bash","# Open extension console","chrome://extensions → Roam → Inspect views → service worker","","# View popup console","Click extension icon → Right click → Inspect popup","","# Check persistent background state","chrome://extensions → Roam → Background Page → Console","```","","---","","## API Reference","","All Edge Functions accept POST requests with JSON bodies. Authentication uses Supabase bearer token in the `Authorization` header.","","### Discovery (`roam`)","","```bash","POST https://project.supabase.co/functions/v1/roam","Authorization: Bearer YOUR_ACCESS_TOKEN","Content-Type: application/json","","{","  \"category_filter\": [\"science\", \"tech\"],      // optional, user's selected categories","  \"exclude_domain\": \"reddit.com\"                 // optional, skip this domain","}","","Response:","{","  \"id\": \"uuid\",","  \"url\": \"https://...\",","  \"title\": \"Page Title\",","  \"description\": \"...\",","  \"category_id\": \"science\",","  \"og_image_url\": \"https://...\",","  \"wilson_score\": 4.5","}","```","","### Rating (`rate`)","","```bash","POST https://project.supabase.co/functions/v1/rate","Authorization: Bearer YOUR_ACCESS_TOKEN","","{","  \"url_id\": \"uuid\",","  \"vote\": 1                  // 1 for thumbs up, -1 for thumbs down","}","","Response:","{","  \"ok\": true,","  \"vote_recorded\": true,","  \"domain_muted\": false      // true if this was the 2nd downvote on this domain","}","```","","### Submission (`submit-url`)","","```bash","POST https://project.supabase.co/functions/v1/submit-url","Authorization: Bearer YOUR_ACCESS_TOKEN","","{","  \"url\": \"https://...\",","  \"category_id\": \"science\"","}","","Response:","{","  \"ok\": true,","  \"id\": \"uuid\",              // moderation queue entry ID","  \"status\": \"pending_review\"","}","```","","### Collections (`collection`)","","```bash","POST https://project.supabase.co/functions/v1/collection","Authorization: Bearer YOUR_ACCESS_TOKEN","","{","  \"action\": \"create\" | \"add_item\" | \"list\" | \"delete\",","  \"name\": \"My Collection\",   // for create","  \"collection_id\": \"uuid\",   // for add_item / delete","  \"url_id\": \"uuid\"           // for add_item","}","","Response (list):","{","  \"ok\": true,","  \"collections\": [","    {\"id\": \"uuid\", \"name\": \"...\", \"item_count\": 5, \"public\": true}","  ]","}","```","","### User Profile (`profile`)","","```bash","GET https://project.supabase.co/functions/v1/profile","Authorization: Bearer YOUR_ACCESS_TOKEN","","Response:","{","  \"user_id\": \"uuid\",","  \"email\": \"user@example.com\",","  \"categories\": [\"science\", \"tech\"],","  \"follower_count\": 42,","  \"following_count\": 18,","  \"urls_submitted\": 5,","  \"urls_rated\": 120","}","```","","---","","## Performance Considerations","","### Discovery Algorithm Efficiency","","The `roam()` function uses several optimization techniques:","","1. **Pre-filtered candidate pool** — only candidates matching user's categories are considered","2. **Domain muting optimization** — muted domains are excluded at query time","3. **Statistical ranking (Wilson score)** — prevents new/popular bias; pages with 5 thumbs-up and 100 votes rank correctly","4. **Efficient sampling** — uses PostgreSQL `TABLESAMPLE BERNOULLI` for large tables","5. **Connection pooling** — Supabase manages connection reuse automatically","","**Expected latency:**","- p50: ~100ms","- p95: ~500ms","- p99: ~2s (when database is under high load)","","### Extension Performance","","- **Queue prefetch** — fetches next 3 URLs in background, instant response to user clicks","- **Lazy script loading** — content scripts load only on tabs with matching domain patterns","- **Storage optimization** — queue stored compressed in chrome.storage.local","- **Minimal permissions** — extension only accesses storage and makes network requests","","**Expected memory usage:**","- Idle: ~5 MB","- Active: ~15 MB","","### Database Scalability","","PostgreSQL free tier supports:","- Millions of URLs (500 MB limit)","- Thousands of concurrent users","- Queries returning 1000+ results easily","","At 100K active monthly users:","- ~5 MB/month database growth (conservative estimate)","- Database will reach 500 MB in ~8-10 years","- No index or query optimization needed until ~1M URLs","","---","","## Monitoring \u0026 Observability","","### Sentry Error Tracking","","All platforms send errors to Sentry for monitoring:","","```bash","# Extension","@sentry/esbuild-plugin uploads source maps on build","All uncaught exceptions captured automatically","","# Web","API errors logged to Sentry with context","User authentication errors tracked separately","","# Android","Kotlin crashes sent to Sentry","ANR (Application Not Responding) events tracked","```","","**Sentry Dashboard:** https://7-lynx.sentry.io/projects/roam-extension","","### Database Monitoring","","Supabase provides free monitoring:","","- **Query performance** — slow query dashboard in Supabase console","- **Storage usage** — monitor database size growth","- **Function invocations** — track Edge Function usage","- **Realtime connections** — see active connections","","**Dashboard:** https://app.supabase.com/project/[YOUR_PROJECT]/analytics","","### Analytics","","Track user engagement and platform health:","","- **URL submission rate** — how many URLs are submitted per day","- **Active users** — daily and monthly active user count","- **Category popularity** — which interest categories are most popular","- **Vote sentiment** — thumbs-up vs thumbs-down ratio per category","","---","","## Contributing","","Contributions are welcome! Areas needing help:","","1. **Bug fixes** — check [Issues](https://github.com/seito/roam/issues) for known bugs","2. **Performance** — profile and optimize slow functions","3. **UI/UX** — improve extension and web interfaces","4. **Documentation** — expand API docs, add tutorials","5. **Testing** — add unit and integration tests","6. **Moderation** — help review and approve URL submissions","","**Getting started:**","","```bash","# Fork the repo","git clone https://github.com/your-username/roam.git","cd roam","","# Create a feature branch","git checkout -b feature/my-improvement","","# Make changes and test","# ...","","# Commit with descriptive message","git commit -m \"feat: add new discovery filter\"","","# Push and open pull request","git push origin feature/my-improvement","```","","**Code style:**","","- Use TypeScript for type safety","- Format code with Prettier (`pnpm format`)","- Run linter: `pnpm lint`","- Write tests for new features","","---","","## License","","MIT License — see [LICENSE](LICENSE) file for details.","","You're free to use, modify, and distribute Roam. Open an issue on GitHub or reach out on Twitter [@seito_codes](https://twitter.com/seito_codes)","","","# View popup console","Click extension icon → Right click → Inspect popup","```","","### Web Platform","","**Development:**","","```bash","cd web","","# Install dependencies","pnpm install","","# Set environment variables","cat \u003e .env.local \u003c\u003c EOF","NEXT_PUBLIC_SUPABASE_URL=https://your-project.supabase.co","NEXT_PUBLIC_SUPABASE_ANON_KEY=your-anon-key","EOF","","# Run dev server","pnpm dev","","# Build for production","pnpm build","pnpm start","","# Run type checking","pnpm type-check","","# Run linter","pnpm lint","```","","**Deployment to Vercel:**","","```bash","# Push to GitHub","git push origin main","","# Vercel auto-deploys on push (if connected)","# Or manually: vercel deploy","```","","### Android App","","**Prerequisites:**","","- Android Studio Ladybug or newer","- Android SDK 26+ (API level)","- Physical device or emulator","","**Setup:**","","1. Open Android Studio","2. Open project folder: `File → Open → select roam/android`","3. Create `local.properties` in `android/` folder:","","```properties","SUPABASE_URL=https://your-project.supabase.co","SUPABASE_ANON_KEY=your-anon-key","SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY=your-service-role-key","```","","4. Sync Gradle: `File → Sync Now`","5. Run on emulator: `Run → Run 'app'`","6. Select target device (emulator or physical device)","","**Building for distribution:**","","```bash","# From Android Studio","Build → Generate Signed Bundle / APK","","# Or from command line","./gradlew assembleRelease  # Creates APK","./gradlew bundleRelease    # Creates AAB for Play Store","```","","### Environment Variables","","**Extension** — stored in chrome.storage.local at runtime","- Auth token (obtained via Supabase OAuth flow)","- Queue state (prefetched URLs)","","**Web** — `.env.local` (never commit this)","```","NEXT_PUBLIC_SUPABASE_URL=https://xyz.supabase.co","NEXT_PUBLIC_SUPABASE_ANON_KEY=eyJ...","DATABASE_URL=postgres://... (server-side only)","```","","**Android** — `local.properties` (never commit this)","```","SUPABASE_URL=https://xyz.supabase.co","SUPABASE_ANON_KEY=eyJ...","```","","**Supabase Secrets** — used by Edge Functions","","```bash","supabase secrets set SENTRY_DSN=https://...","supabase secrets set STRIPE_API_KEY=sk_...","```","","View secrets:","```bash","supabase secrets list","```","","### Running Tests","","```bash","# Extension unit tests (if configured)","cd extension \u0026\u0026 pnpm test","","# Web unit tests","cd web \u0026\u0026 pnpm test","","# Android unit tests","cd android \u0026\u0026 ./gradlew test","","# Android instrumented tests (emulator)","cd android \u0026\u0026 ./gradlew connectedAndroidTest","```","","### Troubleshooting","","**\"Unauthorized\" from Supabase**","- Check anon key is set in `.env.local` / extension config","- Verify Row-Level Security policies in Supabase dashboard","","**Extension not detecting current page**","- Check content script `callback.ts` is loaded: DevTools → Sources → Extensions","- Verify `manifest.json` includes correct `content_scripts` entry","- Reload extension with F5","","**Android app won't build**","- Run `./gradlew clean` to clear cache","- Check SDK levels: `Build → Sync Native Modules`","- Verify `local.properties` has correct Supabase credentials","","**Queue not refilling**","- Check background service worker is running: `chrome://serviceworkers`","- Look for errors in DevTools console (Roam → Inspect views → service worker)","- Verify network connectivity (queue fetch requires Supabase access)"],"stylingDirectives":null,"colorizedLines":["\u003cspan class=\"pl-mh\"\u003e# \u003cspan class=\"pl-en\"\u003eRoam — Rediscover the Web\u003c/span\u003e\u003c/span\u003e","","\u003cspan class=\"pl-ent\"\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-ent\"\u003e\u0026gt; \u003c/span\u003eOne button. Infinite web.\u003c/span\u003e","\u003cspan class=\"pl-ent\"\u003e\u003c/span\u003e","Roam is a community-driven web discovery platform that helps you find genuinely interesting pages matched to your interests. Press one button and land on a curated page voted on by real users, not ranked by engagement metrics or algorithmic preference.","","\u003cspan class=\"pl-ms\"\u003e---\u003c/span\u003e","","\u003cspan class=\"pl-mh\"\u003e## \u003cspan class=\"pl-en\"\u003eWhat is Roam?\u003c/span\u003e\u003c/span\u003e","","The internet is vast and wonderful, but discovery has been broken for years. Algorithmic feeds optimize for engagement, not quality. Bookmarking services feel like digital graveyards. Search requires knowing what you\u0026#39;re looking for.","","Roam brings back the joy of stumbling — the simple act of pressing a button and landing somewhere unexpected that still aligns with what you care about. No feeds. No algorithms optimized to keep you scrolling. No noise. Just real pages, real user votes, and real serendipity.","","\u003cspan class=\"pl-ms\"\u003e---\u003c/span\u003e","","\u003cspan class=\"pl-mh\"\u003e## \u003cspan class=\"pl-en\"\u003eWhy Roam?\u003c/span\u003e\u003c/span\u003e","","The problem with existing discovery tools:","\u003cspan class=\"pl-v\"\u003e-\u003c/span\u003e \u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003e**\u003c/span\u003eAlgorithmic feeds\u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003e**\u003c/span\u003e — optimize for time-on-site, not quality","\u003cspan class=\"pl-v\"\u003e-\u003c/span\u003e \u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003e**\u003c/span\u003eSearch engines\u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003e**\u003c/span\u003e — require you to know what you\u0026#39;re looking for","\u003cspan class=\"pl-v\"\u003e-\u003c/span\u003e \u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003e**\u003c/span\u003eStatic curated lists\u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003e**\u003c/span\u003e — curators can\u0026#39;t match millions of user interests","\u003cspan class=\"pl-v\"\u003e-\u003c/span\u003e \u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003e**\u003c/span\u003eRandom links\u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003e**\u003c/span\u003e — no personalization, no quality signal","\u003cspan class=\"pl-v\"\u003e-\u003c/span\u003e \u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003e**\u003c/span\u003eSocial media\u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003e**\u003c/span\u003e — feeds designed for engagement, not discovery","","Roam solves this with:","\u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003e1\u003c/span\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-v\"\u003e.\u003c/span\u003e \u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003e**\u003c/span\u003eCommunity voting\u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003e**\u003c/span\u003e — real users rate pages, not algorithms","\u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003e**\u003c/span\u003e — your interests control what you see","\u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003e3\u003c/span\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-v\"\u003e.\u003c/span\u003e \u003cspan Features\u003c/span\u003e\u003c/span\u003e","","\u003cspan class=\"pl-mh\"\u003e### \u003cspan class=\"pl-en\"\u003eDiscovery Engine\u003c/span\u003e\u003c/span\u003e","The heart of Roam is the discovery algorithm. Unlike recommendation engines that optimize for engagement:","","\u003cspan class=\"pl-v\"\u003e-\u003c/span\u003e \u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003e**\u003c/span\u003eRoam button\u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003e**\u003c/span\u003e — one tap/click delivers a curated page matching your active interest filters","\u003cspan class=\"pl-v\"\u003e-\u003c/span\u003e \u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003e**\u003c/span\u003eInterest categories\u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003e**\u003c/span\u003e — select categories at signup and adjust at any time (Arts, Science, Tech, News, Gaming, etc.)","\u003cspan class=\"pl-v\"\u003e-\u003c/span\u003e \u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003e**\u003c/span\u003eCommunity-voted ranking\u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003e**\u003c/span\u003e — pages are ranked by real user votes, not engagement metrics","\u003cspan class=\"pl-v\"\u003e-\u003c/span\u003e \u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003e**\u003c/span\u003eDomain diversity\u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003e**\u003c/span\u003e — discovery engine spreads results across different domains to avoid filter bubbles","\u003cspan class=\"pl-v\"\u003e-\u003c/span\u003e \u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003e**\u003c/span\u003ePersonalized pool\u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003e**\u003c/span\u003e — each user gets a candidate pool pre-filtered to their interests, then a single page is randomly selected and served","\u003cspan class=\"pl-v\"\u003e-\u003c/span\u003e \u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003e**\u003c/span\u003eWilson score ranking\u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003e**\u003c/span\u003e — community votes use statistical methods to surface genuinely good content, not just popular content","\u003cspan class=\"pl-v\"\u003e-\u003c/span\u003e \u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003e**\u003c/span\u003eContextual discovery\u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003e**\u003c/span\u003e — \u0026quot;Roam within site\u0026quot; mode lets you stay on a specific domain and discover more pages from it","\u003cspan class=\"pl-v\"\u003e-\u003c/span\u003e \u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003e**\u003c/span\u003eLanguage filtering\u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003e**\u003c/span\u003e — filter results by language preference","\u003cspan class=\"pl-v\"\u003e-\u003c/span\u003e \u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003e**\u003c/span\u003eAdult content filter\u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003e**\u003c/span\u003e — option to exclude NSFW content","","\u003cspan class=\"pl-mh\"\u003e### \u003cspan class=\"pl-en\"\u003eTransparent Personalization\u003c/span\u003e\u003c/span\u003e","","Your recommendations are controlled by just two mechanisms:","","\u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003e1\u003c/span\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-v\"\u003e.\u003c/span\u003e \u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003e**\u003c/span\u003eTopic affinity\u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003e**\u003c/span\u003e — topics you thumbs-up more often appear slightly more often in your results (up to +30% weight boost). Topics you down-vote are not hidden — just weighted a little less, preserving serendipity.","","\u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003e2\u003c/span\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-v\"\u003e.\u003c/span\u003e \u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003e**\u003c/span\u003eDomain muting\u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003e**\u003c/span\u003e — if you thumbs-down 2 or more pages from the same website, that site is automatically muted for 30 days. No manual blocklist to manage; the mute expires on its own.","","Community-level filtering also applies:","\u003cspan class=\"pl-v\"\u003e-\u003c/span\u003e Pages with sustained negative votes from the whole user base are deprioritized for everyone","\u003cspan class=\"pl-v\"\u003e-\u003c/span\u003e Spam and duplicates are removed by moderators","\u003cspan class=\"pl-v\"\u003e-\u003c/span\u003e class="markdown-body entry-content container-lg" itemprop="text"><div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h1 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Roam — Rediscover the Web</h1><a id="user-content-roam--rediscover-the-web" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Roam — Rediscover the Web" href="#roam--rediscover-the-web"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<blockquote>
<p dir="auto">One button. Infinite web.</p>
</blockquote>
<p dir="auto">Roam is a community-driven web discovery platform that helps you find genuinely interesting pages matched to your interests. Press one button and land on a curated page voted on by real users, not ranked by engagement metrics or algorithmic preference.</p>
<hr>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">What is Roam?</h2><a id="user-content-what-is-roam" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: What is Roam?" href="#what-is-roam"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">The internet is vast and wonderful, but discovery has been broken for years. Algorithmic feeds optimize for engagement, not quality. Bookmarking services feel like digital graveyards. Search requires knowing what you're looking for.</p>
<p dir="auto">Roam brings back the joy of stumbling — the simple act of pressing a button and landing somewhere unexpected that still aligns with what you care about. No feeds. No algorithms optimized to keep you scrolling. No noise. Just real pages, real user votes, and real serendipity.</p>
<hr>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Why Roam?</h2><a id="user-content-why-roam" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Why Roam?" href="#why-roam"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">The problem with existing discovery tools:</p>
<ul dir="auto">
<li><strong>Algorithmic feeds</strong> — optimize for time-on-site, not quality</li>
<li><strong>Search engines</strong> — require you to know what you're looking for</li>
<li><strong>Static curated lists</strong> — curators can't match millions of user interests</li>
<li><strong>Random links</strong> — no personalization, no quality signal</li>
<li><strong>Social media</strong> — feeds designed for engagement, not discovery</li>
</ul>
<p dir="auto">Roam solves this with:</p>
<ol dir="auto">
<li><strong>Community voting</strong> — real users rate pages, not algorithms</li>
<li><strong>Transparent personalization</strong> — your interests control what you see</li>
<li><strong>Quality filter</strong> — pages with sustained negative votes are deprioritized</li>
<li><strong>Fresh content</strong> — users continuously submit new URLs</li>
<li><strong>Multi-platform</strong> — web, mobile, and browser extension all sync</li>
<li><strong>Zero ads, zero tracking</strong> — Roam doesn't profit from your attention</li>
</ol>
<hr>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Platform Status</h2><a id="user-content-platform-status" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Platform Status" href="#platform-status"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
