<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Core Features</h2><a id="user-content-core-features" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Core Features" href="#core-features"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Discovery Engine</h3><a id="user-content-discovery-engine" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Discovery Engine" href="#discovery-engine"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">The heart of Roam is the discovery algorithm. Unlike recommendation engines that optimize for engagement:</p>
<ul dir="auto">
<li><strong>Roam button</strong> — one tap/click delivers a curated page matching your active interest filters</li>
<li><strong>Interest categories</strong> — select categories at signup and adjust at any time (Arts, Science, Tech, News, Gaming, etc.)</li>
<li><strong>Community-voted ranking</strong> — pages are ranked by real user votes, not engagement metrics</li>
<li><strong>Domain diversity</strong> — discovery engine spreads results across different domains to avoid filter bubbles</li>
<li><strong>Personalized pool</strong> — each user gets a candidate pool pre-filtered to their interests, then a single page is randomly selected and served</li>
<li><strong>Wilson score ranking</strong> — community votes use statistical methods to surface genuinely good content, not just popular content</li>
<li><strong>Contextual discovery</strong> — "Roam within site" mode lets you stay on a specific domain and discover more pages from it</li>
<li><strong>Language filtering</strong> — filter results by language preference</li>
<li><strong>Adult content filter</strong> — option to exclude NSFW content</li>
</ul>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Transparent Personalization</h3><a id="user-content-transparent-personalization" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Transparent Personalization" href="#transparent-personalization"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">Your recommendations are controlled by just two mechanisms:</p>
<ol dir="auto">
<li>
<p dir="auto"><strong>Topic affinity</strong> — topics you thumbs-up more often appear slightly more often in your results (up to +30% weight boost). Topics you down-vote are not hidden — just weighted a little less, preserving serendipity.</p>
</li>
<li>
<p dir="auto"><strong>Domain muting</strong> — if you thumbs-down 2 or more pages from the same website, that site is automatically muted for 30 days. No manual blocklist to manage; the mute expires on its own.</p>
</li>
</ol>
<p dir="auto">Community-level filtering also applies:</p>
<ul dir="auto">
<li>Pages with sustained negative votes from the whole user base are deprioritized for everyone</li>
<li>Spam and duplicates are removed by moderators</li>
<li>Paywalled content can be flagged and deprioritized</li>
</ul>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Content Management</h3><a id="user-content-content-management" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Content Management" href="#content-management"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">Users drive the content pool:</p>
<ul dir="auto">
<li><strong>URL submission</strong> — any user can submit a new page to Roam</li>
<li><strong>Category tagging</strong> — submitters tag pages with relevant categories</li>
<li><strong>Moderator queue</strong> — submissions are queued for community review</li>
<li><strong>Duplicate detection</strong> — system prevents the same URL from being added twice</li>
<li><strong>Domain tracking</strong> — track which domains contribute most pages and which have lowest user satisfaction</li>
<li><strong>Organic growth</strong> — content pool expands only through user contributions</li>
</ul>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">User Accounts &amp; Profiles</h3><a id="user-content-user-accounts--profiles" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: User Accounts &amp; Profiles" href="#user-accounts--profiles"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<ul dir="auto">
<li><strong>Authentication</strong> — email + password or Google OAuth</li>
<li><strong>Interest profile</strong> — select categories that match your interests</li>
<li><strong>Discovery history</strong> — track what pages you've already rated</li>
<li><strong>Personal blocklist</strong> — manually mute domains for 30 days</li>
<li><strong>Saved collections</strong> — create public or private collections to save pages</li>
<li><strong>User profile</strong> — see your stats: pages rated, collections created, followers</li>
<li><strong>Account settings</strong> — manage auth, privacy, notifications</li>
</ul>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Social Layer</h3><a id="user-content-social-layer" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Social Layer" href="#social-layer"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<ul dir="auto">
<li><strong>Follow other users</strong> — see their activity and recommendation history</li>
<li><strong>Shared collections</strong> — create public collections to share with followers</li>
<li><strong>User profiles</strong> — browse what pages other users have rated highly</li>
<li><strong>Activity notifications</strong> — get notified when users you follow like a page you submitted</li>
<li><strong>Reputation</strong> — track how many followers you have and pages you've contributed</li>
</ul>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Browser Extension</h3><a id="user-content-browser-extension" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Browser Extension" href="#browser-extension"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">Purpose-built for the discovery workflow:</p>
<ul dir="auto">
<li><strong>Non-intrusive design</strong> — click popup, roam, rate, close. Nothing is injected into pages.</li>
<li><strong>Roam button</strong> — get a new page instantly</li>
<li><strong>Thumbs up / down</strong> — rate pages while browsing</li>
<li><strong>Category/collection controls</strong> — save pages to collections or filter by category</li>
<li><strong>Browser-tab integration</strong> — detects and rates the URL you're currently viewing</li>
<li><strong>Queue prefetching</strong> — fetches next 3 pages in background for instant loading</li>
<li><strong>Offline support</strong> — works even if you lose network momentarily</li>
<li><strong>Chrome (MV3) + Firefox (MV3)</strong> — compatible with modern browser extension standards</li>
<li><strong>Automatic updates</strong> — stays in sync with web platform (ratings, follows, collections)</li>
</ul>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Android App</h3><a id="user-content-android-app" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Android App" href="#android-app"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">Optimized for mobile discovery:</p>
<ul dir="auto">
<li><strong>In-app browser</strong> — read pages directly in the app without leaving</li>
<li><strong>Swipe gestures</strong> — swipe right to like, swipe left to skip, swipe down for details</li>
<li><strong>Quick-add to collections</strong> — save pages with one tap</li>
<li><strong>Offline reading queue</strong> — save pages for later without network</li>
<li><strong>Push notifications</strong> — get notified of activity (followers, collections, submissions)</li>
<li><strong>Dark mode</strong> — eye-friendly nighttime reading</li>
<li><strong>Android 8.0+ support</strong> — works on older devices</li>
<li><strong>Material Design 3</strong> — native Android look and feel using Jetpack Compose</li>
</ul>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Web Platform</h3><a id="user-content-web-platform" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Web Platform" href="#web-platform"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">Full-featured discovery and account management:</p>
<ul dir="auto">
<li><strong>Discovery interface</strong> — web version of roam, with advanced filters</li>
<li><strong>Account management</strong> — edit profile, interests, collections, followers</li>
<li><strong>URL submission</strong> — submit new pages directly</li>
<li><strong>Moderation dashboard</strong> — (admin) review and approve submissions</li>
<li><strong>Analytics dashboard</strong> — view platform stats and trends</li>
<li><strong>Responsive design</strong> — works on desktop and tablet</li>
</ul>
<hr>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Architecture &amp; Tech Stack</h2><a id="user-content-architecture--tech-stack" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Architecture &amp; Tech Stack" href="#architecture--tech-stack"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Backend Architecture — Supabase (PostgreSQL + Edge Functions)</h3><a id="user-content-backend-architecture--supabase-postgresql--edge-functions" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Backend Architecture — Supabase (PostgreSQL + Edge Functions)" href="#backend-architecture--supabase-postgresql--edge-functions"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">Built entirely on Supabase's free tier for cost-effective, serverless operation:</p>
<p dir="auto"><strong>Database Layer</strong></p>
<ul dir="auto">
<li><strong>PostgreSQL</strong> — Supabase free tier (500 MB storage, sufficient for millions of discovery records)</li>
<li><strong>Row-Level Security (RLS)</strong> — all access control enforced at the database level; no application-layer auth needed</li>
<li><strong>Key tables:</strong>
<ul dir="auto">
<li><code>urls</code> — discovered pages (url, title, description, category, community votes, wilson_score)</li>
<li><code>collections</code> — user-created collections (name, description, visibility, owner)</li>
<li><code>collection_items</code> — pages saved to collections</li>
<li><code>profiles</code> — user profiles (email, interests, settings, follower count)</li>
<li><code>follows</code> — follow relationships between users</li>
<li><code>ratings</code> — user votes (url_id, user_id, vote direction, timestamp)</li>
<li><code>moderation_queue</code> — submitted URLs pending approval</li>
<li><code>seen_urls</code> — track which pages a user has already rated (prevents duplicates)</li>
</ul>
</li>
</ul>
<p dir="auto"><strong>Authentication</strong></p>
<ul dir="auto">
<li><strong>Supabase Auth</strong> — email/password + Google OAuth out-of-box</li>
<li><strong>Session persistence</strong> — browser-based session storage with auto-refresh</li>
<li><strong>Admin role</strong> — users with <code>is_admin=true</code> can moderate submissions</li>
</ul>
<p dir="auto"><strong>Server-Side Logic — Edge Functions (Deno)</strong></p>
<ul dir="auto">
<li>
<p dir="auto"><strong>roam(category_filter?, exclude_domain?)</strong> — core algorithm RPC function:</p>
<ul dir="auto">
<li>Filters candidate pool by user's categories and muted domains</li>
<li>Applies personalization weights (topic affinity + domain muting)</li>
<li>Samples from remaining candidates using Wilson score ranking</li>
<li>Returns single URL with metadata (title, description, category, OG image)</li>
<li>Falls back gracefully when pool is exhausted</li>
</ul>
</li>
<li>
<p dir="auto"><strong>rate(url_id, vote)</strong> — record user votes:</p>
<ul dir="auto">
<li>Validates URL ownership and user permissions</li>
<li>Updates vote count and recalculates Wilson score</li>
<li>Records in <code>ratings</code> table for personalization</li>
<li>Triggers domain-mute logic if threshold reached</li>
</ul>
</li>
<li>
<p dir="auto"><strong>submit-url(url, category)</strong> — submit new pages:</p>
<ul dir="auto">
<li>Normalizes URL (removes tracking params, www prefix, fragments)</li>
<li>Checks for duplicates</li>
<li>Adds to moderation queue</li>
<li>Notifies moderators</li>
</ul>
</li>
<li>
<p dir="auto"><strong>collection(action, ...)</strong> — manage saved pages:</p>
<ul dir="auto">
<li>Create collections</li>
<li>Add/remove URLs from collections</li>
<li>List collections with item counts</li>
<li>Share collections publicly</li>
</ul>
</li>
<li>
<p dir="auto"><strong>follow(action, user_id)</strong> — manage follows:</p>
<ul dir="auto">
<li>Follow/unfollow users</li>
<li>List followers and following</li>
<li>Calculate follower counts for profiles</li>
</ul>
</li>
<li>
<p dir="auto"><strong>log-failed-urls(failed_urls)</strong> — batch error logging:</p>
<ul dir="auto">
<li>Records URLs that failed validation (for moderation)</li>
<li>Implements retry logic with exponential backoff</li>
<li>Captures to Sentry for monitoring</li>
</ul>
</li>
</ul>
<p dir="auto"><strong>API Gateway &amp; CORS</strong></p>
<ul dir="auto">
<li>All Edge Functions use shared CORS headers (<code>_shared/cors.ts</code>)</li>
<li>Authentication via Supabase bearer token in <code>Authorization</code> header</li>
<li>All endpoints are POST or POST+OPTIONS (for CORS preflight)</li>
</ul>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Browser Extension (<code>/extension</code>)</h3><a id="user-content-browser-extension-extension" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Browser Extension (/extension)" href="#browser-extension-extension"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto"><strong>Architecture</strong></p>
<ul dir="auto">
<li><strong>Manifest V3</strong> — modern extension standard, works on Chrome and Firefox</li>
<li><strong>Service Worker</strong> — background.ts handles all logic:
<ul dir="auto">
<li>Persistent storage of auth state and queue</li>
<li>Message routing from popup and content scripts</li>
<li>Background sync (queue refilling, failed URL retries)</li>
</ul>
</li>
<li><strong>Popup UI</strong> — popup.ts handles user interface</li>
<li><strong>Content Script</strong> — callback.ts detects current page and enables rating</li>
</ul>
<p dir="auto"><strong>Build &amp; Distribution</strong></p>
<ul dir="auto">
<li><strong>Build tool</strong> — esbuild with TypeScript</li>
<li><strong>Source maps</strong> — uploaded to Sentry with @sentry/esbuild-plugin</li>
<li><strong>Chrome</strong> — Chrome Web Store ($5 one-time)</li>
<li><strong>Firefox</strong> — Firefox Add-ons (free)</li>
<li><strong>Sentry integration</strong> — monitors crashes and errors from all users</li>
</ul>
<p dir="auto"><strong>Queue Management</strong></p>
<ul dir="auto">
<li><strong>Hot queue</strong> — 3 pre-fetched URLs ready to display instantly</li>
<li><strong>Warming queue</strong> — 5 additional URLs being fetched in background</li>
<li><strong>Prefetch loop</strong> — continuously maintains queue above threshold</li>
<li><strong>Persistent storage</strong> — chrome.storage.local survives restarts</li>
<li><strong>Retry logic</strong> — failed fetches retry with exponential backoff</li>
</ul>
<p dir="auto"><strong>Key Modules</strong></p>
<ul dir="auto">
<li><code>background.ts</code> — service worker, message dispatcher, auth handler</li>
<li><code>popup.ts</code> — UI logic, roam button, rating buttons, category filter</li>
<li><code>callback.ts</code> — detects current page, enables rating of known URLs</li>
<li><code>lib/queue.ts</code> — queue state management and batch retry</li>
<li><code>lib/queueManager.ts</code> — background loops for validation and refill</li>
<li><code>lib/supabase.ts</code> — Supabase client factory with auth state</li>
</ul>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Android App (<code>/android</code>)</h3><a id="user-content-android-app-android" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Android App (/android)" href="#android-app-android"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto"><strong>Language &amp; UI</strong></p>
<ul dir="auto">
<li><strong>Kotlin</strong> — type-safe, concise Android development</li>
<li><strong>Jetpack Compose</strong> — modern declarative UI framework</li>
<li><strong>Material Design 3</strong> — latest Android design system with theming</li>
<li><strong>Navigation Compose</strong> — type-safe navigation between screens</li>
</ul>
<p dir="auto"><strong>Networking &amp; Authentication</strong></p>
<ul dir="auto">
<li><strong>Supabase Kotlin client</strong> — official SDK for database and auth</li>
<li><strong>Google Sign-In</strong> — integrated into Supabase Auth</li>
<li><strong>Session persistence</strong> — auto-refresh tokens via Supabase</li>
</ul>
<p dir="auto"><strong>Key Features</strong></p>
<ul dir="auto">
<li><strong>In-app WebView</strong> — render discovered pages within the app</li>
<li><strong>Swipe gestures</strong> — intuitive right (like) / left (skip) / down (details) interactions</li>
<li><strong>Offline queue</strong> — save pages with content snapshot for offline reading</li>
<li><strong>Push notifications</strong> — Firebase Cloud Messaging for activity alerts</li>
<li><strong>Min SDK 26 (Android 8.0)</strong> — supports older devices</li>
<li><strong>Target SDK 34 (Android 14)</strong> — modern API features</li>
</ul>
<p dir="auto"><strong>Key Screens</strong></p>
<ul dir="auto">
<li>Roam screen — display current URL, swipe controls, metadata</li>
<li>Collections screen — create and manage saved page collections</li>
<li>Profile screen — view account, interests, followers, statistics</li>
<li>Following screen — discover and follow other users</li>
<li>Settings screen — auth, notifications, content filtering</li>
</ul>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Web Platform (<code>/web</code>)</h3><a id="user-content-web-platform-web" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Web Platform (/web)" href="#web-platform-web"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto"><strong>Framework &amp; Build</strong></p>
<ul dir="auto">
<li><strong>Next.js 15</strong> — React framework with server-side rendering</li>
<li><strong>TypeScript</strong> — type-safe full-stack code</li>
<li><strong>Tailwind CSS</strong> — utility-first styling</li>
<li><strong>Vercel deployment</strong> — free tier hosting with auto-deployment on git push</li>
</ul>
<p dir="auto"><strong>Key Pages</strong></p>
<ul dir="auto">
<li>Home — roam interface with discovery</li>
<li>Profile — view user account and statistics</li>
<li>Collections — browse public collections</li>
<li>Submit URL — contribute new pages</li>
<li>Moderation (admin) — review and approve submissions</li>
<li>Analytics (admin) — view platform statistics</li>
</ul>
<p dir="auto"><strong>Authentication &amp; State</strong></p>
<ul dir="auto">
<li><strong>Supabase Auth</strong> — user sessions and permissions</li>
<li><strong>Server-side sessions</strong> — secure auth via Supabase SSR library</li>
<li><strong>Client-side persistence</strong> — React Query for state management</li>
</ul>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Database Design Highlights</h3><a id="user-content-database-design-highlights" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Database Design Highlights" href="#database-design-highlights"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto"><strong>Efficient Discovery</strong></p>
<ul dir="auto">
<li><code>urls</code> table indexed on <code>category_id</code> and <code>approved</code> for fast filtering</li>
<li><code>ratings</code> table indexed on <code>url_id</code> for vote aggregation</li>
<li><code>seen_urls</code> tracks viewed pages to prevent repeats</li>
<li>Wilson score algorithm ranks pages statistically correctly (no bias toward new or old)</li>
</ul>
<p dir="auto"><strong>Scalability</strong></p>
<ul dir="auto">
<li>Normalized schema prevents data duplication</li>
<li>RLS policies prevent data leakage between users</li>
<li>Foreign keys enforce referential integrity</li>
<li>Materialized queries cache expensive aggregations (follower counts, collection sizes)</li>
</ul>
<hr>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Repository Structure</h2><a id="user-content-repository-structure" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Repository Structure" href="#repository-structure"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>

│       ├── follow/            # POST /functions/v1/follow — manage follows
│       ├── profile/           # GET /functions/v1/profile — user profile data
│       ├── log-failed-urls/   # POST /functions/v1/log-failed-urls — error tracking
│       └── _shared/           # Shared utilities
│           ├── cors.ts        # CORS headers for all endpoints
│           └── auth.ts        # Auth helper functions
│
├── web/                        # Next.js web application (Vercel)
│   ├── src/
│   │   ├── app/              # Next.js app directory
│   │   │   ├── page.tsx      # Home page (roam interface)
│   │   │   ├── profile/      # User profile pages
│   │   │   ├── collections/  # Collection browsing
│   │   │   └── submit/       # URL submission form
│   │   ├── components/        # React components
│   │   ├── lib/              # Utility functions
│   │   └── middleware.ts      # Auth middleware
│   ├── package.json          # Dependencies and build scripts
│   ├── next.config.ts        # Next.js configuration
│   ├── tsconfig.json         # TypeScript config
│   └── .env.local            # Supabase credentials
│
├── extension/                  # Browser extension (Chrome + Firefox)
│   ├── src/
│   │   ├── background/       # Service worker (background.ts)
│   │   ├── popup/            # Popup UI (popup.ts, popup.css, popup.html)
│   │   ├── callback/         # Content script (callback.ts)
│   │   ├── lib/              # Shared utilities
│   │   │   ├── queue.ts      # Queue state and batch retry
│   │   │   ├── queueManager.ts  # Background loops
│   │   │   └── supabase.ts   # Supabase client factory
│   │   └── types/            # TypeScript type definitions
│   ├── icons/                # Extension icons (16x16, 32x32, 48x48, 128x128)
│   ├── dist/                 # Compiled output (ignored in git)
│   ├── dist-firefox/         # Firefox-specific bundle
│   ├── manifest.json         # Extension manifest (Chrome)
│   ├── manifest.firefox.json # Extension manifest (Firefox)
│   ├── build.mjs             # esbuild configuration
│   ├── package.json          # Dependencies
│   └── tsconfig.json         # TypeScript config
│
├── android/                    # Android app (Kotlin + Compose)
│   ├── app/src/main/kotlin/com/roam
│   │   ├── MainActivity.kt    # Entry point
│   │   ├── ui/               # Jetpack Compose screens
│   │   ├── viewmodel/        # MVVM ViewModels
│   │   └── data/             # Repository and Supabase client
│   ├── build.gradle.kts      # Kotlin DSL build config
│   ├── gradle.properties     # Gradle settings
│   ├── local.properties      # Local secrets (not in git)
│   └── settings.gradle.kts   # Gradle subproject config
│
├── scripts/                    # One-off utility scripts
│   ├── seed-*.js             # Import URLs from public sources (Wikipedia, Hacker News, etc.)
│   ├── _test-*.mjs           # Quick tests of seed scripts
│   └── resize-icons.ps1      # Icon resizing script
│
├── PLANNING.md               # Long-term roadmap
├── ROADMAP.md                # Public build tasks &amp; progress tracking
├── LICENSE                   # MIT license
└── README.md                # This file
</code></pre></div>
<hr>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Development Setup</h2><a id="user-content-development-setup" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Development Setup" href="#development-setup"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Prerequisites</h3><a id="user-content-prerequisites" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Prerequisites" href="#prerequisites"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<ul dir="auto">
<li><strong>Node.js 20+</strong> — for extension build and web platform</li>
<li><strong>pnpm 9+</strong> — fast, space-efficient package manager (or npm/yarn if preferred)</li>
<li><strong>Supabase CLI</strong> — <code>npm install -g supabase</code> or <code>brew install supabase</code></li>
<li><strong>Android Studio</strong> — Ladybug version or newer (for Android development)</li>
<li><strong>Git</strong> — version control</li>
</ul>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Quick Start (5 minutes)</h3><a id="user-content-quick-start-5-minutes" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Quick Start (5 minutes)" href="#quick-start-5-minutes"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="# 1. Clone repository
git clone https://github.com/seito/roam.git
cd roam

# 2. Create free Supabase project at https://supabase.com
# Copy your project URL and anon key

# 3. Backend setup
supabase login
supabase link --project-ref YOUR_PROJECT_REF
supabase db push          # Applies all SQL migrations
supabase functions deploy # Deploys Edge Functions

# 4. Extension setup
cd extension
pnpm install
pnpm build               # Outputs to dist/
# Load unpacked in Chrome: chrome://extensions → Developer mode → Load unpacked → select dist/"><pre><span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> 1. Clone repository</span>
git clone https://github.com/seito/roam.git
<span class="pl-c1">cd</span> roam

<span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> 2. Create free Supabase project at https://supabase.com</span>
<span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> Copy your project URL and anon key</span>

<span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> 3. Backend setup</span>
supabase login
supabase link --project-ref YOUR_PROJECT_REF
supabase db push          <span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> Applies all SQL migrations</span>
supabase functions deploy <span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> Deploys Edge Functions</span>

<span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> 4. Extension setup</span>
<span class="pl-c1">cd</span> extension
pnpm install
pnpm build               <span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> Outputs to dist/</span>
<span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> Load unpacked in Chrome: chrome://extensions → Developer mode → Load unpacked → select dist/</span></pre></div>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Backend (Supabase)</h3><a id="user-content-backend-supabase" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Backend (Supabase)" href="#backend-supabase"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto"><strong>One-time setup:</strong></p>
<ol dir="auto">
<li>Create a free account at <a href="https://supabase.com" rel="nofollow">supabase.com</a></li>
<li>Create a new project (any region)</li>
<li>Get your project URL and anon key from Settings → API Keys</li>
</ol>
<p dir="auto"><strong>Local development:</strong></p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="cd supabase

# Authenticate with Supabase
supabase login

# Link to your project
supabase link --project-ref YOUR_PROJECT_REF

# Apply all database migrations
supabase db push

# Deploy Edge Functions
supabase functions deploy roam
supabase functions deploy rate
supabase functions deploy submit-url
supabase functions deploy collection
supabase functions deploy follow
supabase functions deploy profile
supabase functions deploy log-failed-urls

# Run local dev server (optional)
supabase start  # Starts local PostgreSQL, auth server, and functions
supabase stop"><pre><span class="pl-c1">cd</span> supabase

<span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> Authenticate with Supabase</span>
supabase login

<span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> Link to your project</span>
supabase link --project-ref YOUR_PROJECT_REF

<span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> Apply all database migrations</span>
supabase db push

<span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> Deploy Edge Functions</span>
supabase functions deploy roam
supabase functions deploy rate
supabase functions deploy submit-url
supabase functions deploy collection
supabase functions deploy follow
supabase functions deploy profile
supabase functions deploy log-failed-urls

<span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> Run local dev server (optional)</span>
supabase start  <span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> Starts local PostgreSQL, auth server, and functions</span>
supabase stop</pre></div>
<p dir="auto"><strong>Verifying the backend:</strong></p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="# List deployed functions
supabase functions list

# Check Edge Function logs
supabase functions download roam
supabase edge-functions --help"><pre><span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> List deployed functions</span>
supabase functions list

<span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> Check Edge Function logs</span>
supabase functions download roam
supabase edge-functions --help</pre></div>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Browser Extension</h3><a id="user-content-browser-extension-1" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Browser Extension" href="#browser-extension-1"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto"><strong>Development:</strong></p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="cd extension

# Install dependencies
pnpm install

# Build and watch for changes
pnpm watch

# Or build once
pnpm build

# Output: dist/ (Chrome) and dist-firefox/ (Firefox)"><pre><span class="pl-c1">cd</span> extension

<span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> Install dependencies</span>
pnpm install

<span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> Build and watch for changes</span>
pnpm watch

<span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> Or build once</span>
pnpm build

<span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> Output: dist/ (Chrome) and dist-firefox/ (Firefox)</span></pre></div>
<p dir="auto"><strong>Loading in Chrome:</strong></p>
<ol dir="auto">
<li>Open <code>chrome://extensions</code></li>
<li>Enable "Developer mode" (top right)</li>
<li>Click "Load unpacked"</li>
<li>Select the <code>dist/</code> folder</li>
<li>Extension appears in your toolbar</li>
</ol>
<p dir="auto"><strong>Loading in Firefox:</strong></p>
<ol dir="auto">
<li>Open <code>about:debugging</code></li>
<li>Click "This Firefox"</li>
<li>Click "Load Temporary Add-on"</li>
<li>Select <code>dist-firefox/manifest.json</code></li>
</ol>
<p dir="auto"><strong>Debugging:</strong></p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="# Open extension console
chrome://extensions → Roam → Inspect views → service worker

# View popup console
Click extension icon → Right click → Inspect popup

# Check persistent background state
chrome://extensions → Roam → Background Page → Console"><pre><span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> Open extension console</span>
chrome://extensions → Roam → Inspect views → service worker

<span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> View popup console</span>
Click extension icon → Right click → Inspect popup

<span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> Check persistent background state</span>
chrome://extensions → Roam → Background Page → Console</pre></div>

<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">API Reference</h2><a id="user-content-api-reference" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: API Reference" href="#api-reference"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">All Edge Functions accept POST requests with JSON bodies. Authentication uses Supabase bearer token in the <code>Authorization</code> header.</p>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Discovery (<code>roam</code>)</h3><a id="user-content-discovery-roam" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Discovery (roam)" href="#discovery-roam"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="POST https://project.supabase.co/functions/v1/roam
Authorization: Bearer YOUR_ACCESS_TOKEN
Content-Type: application/json

{
  &quot;category_filter&quot;: [&quot;science&quot;, &quot;tech&quot;],      // optional, user's selected categories
  &quot;exclude_domain&quot;: &quot;reddit.com&quot;                 // optional, skip this domain
}

Response:
{
  &quot;id&quot;: &quot;uuid&quot;,
  &quot;url&quot;: &quot;https://...&quot;,
  &quot;title&quot;: &quot;Page Title&quot;,
  &quot;description&quot;: &quot;...&quot;,
  &quot;category_id&quot;: &quot;science&quot;,
  &quot;og_image_url&quot;: &quot;https://...&quot;,
  &quot;wilson_score&quot;: 4.5
}"><pre>POST https://project.supabase.co/functions/v1/roam
Authorization: Bearer YOUR_ACCESS_TOKEN
Content-Type: application/json

{
  <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">"</span>category_filter<span class="pl-pds">"</span></span>: [<span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">"</span>science<span class="pl-pds">"</span></span>, <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">"</span>tech<span class="pl-pds">"</span></span>],      // optional, user<span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">'</span>s selected categories</span>
<span class="pl-s">  "exclude_domain": "reddit.com"                 // optional, skip this domain</span>
<span class="pl-s">}</span>
<span class="pl-s"></span>
<span class="pl-s">Response:</span>
<span class="pl-s">{</span>
<span class="pl-s">  "id": "uuid",</span>
<span class="pl-s">  "url": "https://...",</span>
<span class="pl-s">  "title": "Page Title",</span>
<span class="pl-s">  "description": "...",</span>
<span class="pl-s">  "category_id": "science",</span>
<span class="pl-s">  "og_image_url": "https://...",</span>
<span class="pl-s">  "wilson_score": 4.5</span>
<span class="pl-s">}</span></pre></div>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Rating (<code>rate</code>)</h3><a id="user-content-rating-rate" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Rating (rate)" href="#rating-rate"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="POST https://project.supabase.co/functions/v1/rate
Authorization: Bearer YOUR_ACCESS_TOKEN

{
  &quot;url_id&quot;: &quot;uuid&quot;,
  &quot;vote&quot;: 1                  // 1 for thumbs up, -1 for thumbs down
}

Response:
{
  &quot;ok&quot;: true,
  &quot;vote_recorded&quot;: true,
  &quot;domain_muted&quot;: false      // true if this was the 2nd downvote on this domain
}"><pre>POST https://project.supabase.co/functions/v1/rate
Authorization: Bearer YOUR_ACCESS_TOKEN

{
  <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">"</span>url_id<span class="pl-pds">"</span></span>: <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">"</span>uuid<span class="pl-pds">"</span></span>,
  <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">"</span>vote<span class="pl-pds">"</span></span>: 1                  // 1 <span class="pl-k">for</span> thumbs up, -1 <span class="pl-k">for</span> thumbs down
}

Response:
{
  <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">"</span>ok<span class="pl-pds">"</span></span>: true,
  <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">"</span>vote_recorded<span class="pl-pds">"</span></span>: true,
  <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">"</span>domain_muted<span class="pl-pds">"</span></span>: <span class="pl-c1">false</span>      // <span class="pl-c1">true</span> <span class="pl-k">if</span> this was the 2nd downvote on this domain
}</pre></div>

<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Performance Considerations</h2><a id="user-content-performance-considerations" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Performance Considerations" href="#performance-considerations"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Discovery Algorithm Efficiency</h3><a id="user-content-discovery-algorithm-efficiency" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Discovery Algorithm Efficiency" href="#discovery-algorithm-efficiency"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">The <code>roam()</code> function uses several optimization techniques:</p>
<ol dir="auto">
<li><strong>Pre-filtered candidate pool</strong> — only candidates matching user's categories are considered</li>
<li><strong>Domain muting optimization</strong> — muted domains are excluded at query time</li>
<li><strong>Statistical ranking (Wilson score)</strong> — prevents new/popular bias; pages with 5 thumbs-up and 100 votes rank correctly</li>
<li><strong>Efficient sampling</strong> — uses PostgreSQL <code>TABLESAMPLE BERNOULLI</code> for large tables</li>
<li><strong>Connection pooling</strong> — Supabase manages connection reuse automatically</li>
</ol>
<p dir="auto"><strong>Expected latency:</strong></p>
<ul dir="auto">
<li>p50: ~100ms</li>
<li>p95: ~500ms</li>
<li>p99: ~2s (when database is under high load)</li>
</ul>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Extension Performance</h3><a id="user-content-extension-performance" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Extension Performance" href="#extension-performance"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<ul dir="auto">
<li><strong>Queue prefetch</strong> — fetches next 3 URLs in background, instant response to user clicks</li>
<li><strong>Lazy script loading</strong> — content scripts load only on tabs with matching domain patterns</li>
<li><strong>Storage optimization</strong> — queue stored compressed in chrome.storage.local</li>
<li><strong>Minimal permissions</strong> — extension only accesses storage and makes network requests</li>
</ul>
<p dir="auto"><strong>Expected memory usage:</strong></p>
<ul dir="auto">
<li>Idle: ~5 MB</li>
<li>Active: ~15 MB</li>
</ul>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Database Scalability</h3><a id="user-content-database-scalability" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Database Scalability" href="#database-scalability"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">PostgreSQL free tier supports:</p>
<ul dir="auto">
<li>Millions of URLs (500 MB limit)</li>
<li>Thousands of concurrent users</li>
<li>Queries returning 1000+ results easily</li>
</ul>
<p dir="auto">At 100K active monthly users:</p>
<ul dir="auto">
<li>~5 MB/month database growth (conservative estimate)</li>
<li>Database will reach 500 MB in ~8-10 years</li>
<li>No index or query optimization needed until ~1M URLs</li>
</ul>
<hr>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Monitoring &amp; Observability</h2><a id="user-content-monitoring--observability" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Monitoring &amp; Observability" href="#monitoring--observability"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Sentry Error Tracking</h3><a id="user-content-sentry-error-tracking" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Sentry Error Tracking" href="#sentry-error-tracking"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">All platforms send errors to Sentry for monitoring:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="# Extension
@sentry/esbuild-plugin uploads source maps on build
All uncaught exceptions captured automatically

# Web
API errors logged to Sentry with context
User authentication errors tracked separately

# Android
Kotlin crashes sent to Sentry
ANR (Application Not Responding) events tracked"><pre><span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> Extension</span>
@sentry/esbuild-plugin uploads <span class="pl-c1">source</span> maps on build
All uncaught exceptions captured automatically

<span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> Web</span>
API errors logged to Sentry with context
User authentication errors tracked separately

<span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> Android</span>
Kotlin crashes sent to Sentry
ANR (Application Not Responding) events tracked</pre></div>
<p dir="auto"><strong>Sentry Dashboard:</strong> <a href="https://7-lynx.sentry.io/projects/roam-extension" rel="nofollow">https://7-lynx.sentry.io/projects/roam-extension</a></p>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Database Monitoring</h3><a id="user-content-database-monitoring" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Database Monitoring" href="#database-monitoring"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">Supabase provides free monitoring:</p>
<ul dir="auto">
<li><strong>Query performance</strong> — slow query dashboard in Supabase console</li>
<li><strong>Storage usage</strong> — monitor database size growth</li>
<li><strong>Function invocations</strong> — track Edge Function usage</li>
<li><strong>Realtime connections</strong> — see active connections</li>
</ul>

<p dir="auto">Contributions are welcome! Areas needing help:</p>
<ol dir="auto">

<li><strong>UI/UX</strong> — improve extension and web interfaces</li>
<li><strong>Documentation</strong> — expand API docs, add tutorials</li>
<li><strong>Testing</strong> — add unit and integration tests</li>
<li><strong>Moderation</strong> — help review and approve URL submissions</li>
</ol>
<p dir="auto"><strong>Getting started:</strong></p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="# Fork the repo
git clone https://github.com/your-username/roam.git
cd roam

# Create a feature branch
git checkout -b feature/my-improvement

# Make changes and test
# ...

# Commit with descriptive message
git commit -m &quot;feat: add new discovery filter&quot;

# Push and open pull request
git push origin feature/my-improvement"><pre><span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> Fork the repo</span>

<span class="pl-c1">cd</span> roam

<span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> Make changes and test</span>

<span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> Push and open pull request</span>

<div class="snippet-clipboard-content notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="
### Web Platform

**Development:**

```bash
cd web

# Install dependencies
pnpm install

# Set environment variables
cat &gt; .env.local &lt;&lt; EOF
NEXT_PUBLIC_SUPABASE_URL=https://your-project.supabase.co
NEXT_PUBLIC_SUPABASE_ANON_KEY=your-anon-key
EOF

# Run dev server
pnpm dev

# Build for production
pnpm build
pnpm start

# Run type checking
pnpm type-check

# Run linter
pnpm lint"><pre class="notranslate"><code>
### Web Platform

**Development:**

```bash
cd web

# Install dependencies
pnpm install

# Set environment variables
cat &gt; .env.local &lt;&lt; EOF
NEXT_PUBLIC_SUPABASE_URL=https://your-project.supabase.co
NEXT_PUBLIC_SUPABASE_ANON_KEY=your-anon-key
EOF

# Run dev server
pnpm dev

# Build for production
pnpm build
pnpm start

# Run type checking
pnpm type-check

# Run linter
pnpm lint
</code></pre></div>
<p dir="auto"><strong>Deployment to Vercel:</strong></p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="# Push to GitHub
git push origin main

# Vercel auto-deploys on push (if connected)
# Or manually: vercel deploy"><pre><span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> Push to GitHub</span>
git push origin main

<span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> Vercel auto-deploys on push (if connected)</span>
<span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> Or manually: vercel deploy</span></pre></div>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Android App</h3><a id="user-content-android-app-1" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Android App" href="#android-app-1"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto"><strong>Prerequisites:</strong></p>
<ul dir="auto">
<li>Android Studio Ladybug or newer</li>
<li>Android SDK 26+ (API level)</li>
<li>Physical device or emulator</li>
</ul>
<p dir="auto"><strong>Setup:</strong></p>
<ol dir="auto">
<li>Open Android Studio</li>
<li>Open project folder: <code>File → Open → select roam/android</code></li>
<li>Create <code>local.properties</code> in <code>android/</code> folder:</li>
</ol>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-ini notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="SUPABASE_URL=https://your-project.supabase.co
SUPABASE_ANON_KEY=your-anon-key
SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY=your-service-role-key"><pre><span class="pl-k">SUPABASE_URL</span>=https://your-project.supabase.co
<span class="pl-k">SUPABASE_ANON_KEY</span>=your-anon-key
<span class="pl-k">SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY</span>=your-service-role-key</pre></div>
<ol start="4" dir="auto">
<li>Sync Gradle: <code>File → Sync Now</code></li>
<li>Run on emulator: <code>Run → Run 'app'</code></li>
<li>Select target device (emulator or physical device)</li>
</ol>
<p dir="auto"><strong>Building for distribution:</strong></p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="# From Android Studio
Build → Generate Signed Bundle / APK

# Or from command line
./gradlew assembleRelease  # Creates APK
./gradlew bundleRelease    # Creates AAB for Play Store"><pre><span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> From Android Studio</span>
Build → Generate Signed Bundle / APK

<span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> Or from command line</span>
./gradlew assembleRelease  <span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> Creates APK</span>
./gradlew bundleRelease    <span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> Creates AAB for Play Store</span></pre></div>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Environment Variables</h3><a id="user-content-environment-variables" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Environment Variables" href="#environment-variables"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto"><strong>Extension</strong> — stored in chrome.storage.local at runtime</p>
<ul dir="auto">
<li>Auth token (obtained via Supabase OAuth flow)</li>
<li>Queue state (prefetched URLs)</li>
</ul>
<p dir="auto"><strong>Web</strong> — <code>.env.local</code> (never commit this)</p>
<div class="snippet-clipboard-content notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="NEXT_PUBLIC_SUPABASE_URL=https://xyz.supabase.co
NEXT_PUBLIC_SUPABASE_ANON_KEY=eyJ...
DATABASE_URL=postgres://... (server-side only)"><pre class="notranslate"><code>NEXT_PUBLIC_SUPABASE_URL=https://xyz.supabase.co
NEXT_PUBLIC_SUPABASE_ANON_KEY=eyJ...
DATABASE_URL=postgres://... (server-side only)
</code></pre></div>
<p dir="auto"><strong>Android</strong> — <code>local.properties</code> (never commit this)</p>
<div class="snippet-clipboard-content notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="SUPABASE_URL=https://xyz.supabase.co
SUPABASE_ANON_KEY=eyJ..."><pre class="notranslate"><code>SUPABASE_URL=https://xyz.supabase.co
SUPABASE_ANON_KEY=eyJ...
</code></pre></div>
<p dir="auto"><strong>Supabase Secrets</strong> — used by Edge Functions</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="supabase secrets set SENTRY_DSN=https://...
supabase secrets set STRIPE_API_KEY=sk_..."><pre>supabase secrets <span class="pl-c1">set</span> SENTRY_DSN=https://...
supabase secrets <span class="pl-c1">set</span> STRIPE_API_KEY=sk_...</pre></div>
<p dir="auto">View secrets:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="supabase secrets list"><pre>supabase secrets list</pre></div>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Running Tests</h3><a id="user-content-running-tests" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Running Tests" href="#running-tests"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="# Extension unit tests (if configured)
cd extension &amp;&amp; pnpm test

# Web unit tests
cd web &amp;&amp; pnpm test

# Android unit tests
cd android &amp;&amp; ./gradlew test

# Android instrumented tests (emulator)
cd android &amp;&amp; ./gradlew connectedAndroidTest"><pre><span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> Extension unit tests (if configured)</span>
<span class="pl-c1">cd</span> extension <span class="pl-k">&amp;&amp;</span> pnpm <span class="pl-c1">test</span>

<span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> Web unit tests</span>
<span class="pl-c1">cd</span> web <span class="pl-k">&amp;&amp;</span> pnpm <span class="pl-c1">test</span>

<span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> Android unit tests</span>
<span class="pl-c1">cd</span> android <span class="pl-k">&amp;&amp;</span> ./gradlew <span class="pl-c1">test</span>

<span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> Android instrumented tests (emulator)</span>
<span class="pl-c1">cd</span> android <span class="pl-k">&amp;&amp;</span> ./gradlew connectedAndroidTest</pre></div>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Troubleshooting</h3><a id="user-content-troubleshooting" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Troubleshooting" href="#troubleshooting"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto"><strong>"Unauthorized" from Supabase</strong></p>
<ul dir="auto">
<li>Check anon key is set in <code>.env.local</code> / extension config</li>
<li>Verify Row-Level Security policies in Supabase dashboard</li>
</ul>
<p dir="auto"><strong>Extension not detecting current page</strong></p>
<ul dir="auto">
<li>Check content script <code>callback.ts</code> is loaded: DevTools → Sources → Extensions</li>
<li>Verify <code>manifest.json</code> includes correct <code>content_scripts</code> entry</li>
<li>Reload extension with F5</li>
</ul>
<p dir="auto"><strong>Android app won't build</strong></p>
<ul dir="auto">
<li>Run <code>./gradlew clean</code> to clear cache</li>
<li>Check SDK levels: <code>Build → Sync Native Modules</code></li>
<li>Verify <code>local.properties</code> has correct Supabase credentials</li>
</ul>
<p dir="auto"><strong>Queue not refilling</strong></p>
<ul dir="auto">
<li>Check background service worker is running: <code>chrome://serviceworkers</code></li>
<li>Look for errors in DevTools console (Roam → Inspect views → service worker)</li>
<li>Verify network connectivity (queue fetch requires Supabase access)</li>
</ul>
</article></div><div class="d-none"></div></section></div></div><div class="BlobViewContent-module__symbolsPanelSpacer__Vuo_9"></div><div class="Panel-module__Box__AdYCI panel-content-narrow-styles     <style>
      .user-mention[href$="/seven-lynx"] {
        color: var(--color-user-mention-fg);
        background-color: var(--bgColor-attention-muted, var(--color-attention-subtle));
        border-radius: 2px;
        margin-left: -2px;
        margin-right: -2px;
      }
      .user-mention[href$="/seven-lynx"]:before,
      .user-mention[href$="/seven-lynx"]:after {
        content: '';
        display: inline-block;
        width: 2px;
      }
    </style>
